Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on proteins structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 814. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 195 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_814(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 814. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^195(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The proteins operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 160. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 920 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 160 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the proteins structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 920. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
